public class TM extends java.lang.Object {
    public TM() {
        // @method <init>()V
        // @declaration a constructor of `TM`, member flags 0x0001
        // recovered from bytecode; presentation is not claimed to compile
        super();
        return;
    }

    public static void main(java.lang.String[] arg0) {

    }

            java.lang.System.out.println("slow-step");
            return;

            // @method hook()V

            java.lang.System.out.println("slow-hook:" + this.label());
            return;
        }
    }

    static abstract class Task extends java.lang.Object {
        private final java.lang.String name;

        protected Task(java.lang.String arg1) {
            // @method <init>(Ljava/lang/String;)V

        abstract void step();

        protected void hook() {
            // @method hook()V

            // recovered from bytecode; presentation is not claimed to compile
            java.lang.System.out.println("default-hook");
            return;

            // recovered from bytecode; presentation is not claimed to compile
            java.lang.System.out.println((java.lang.String) new java.lang.StringBuilder().append("begin:").append(this.name).toString());
            this.step();

            return;
        }

            // @method label()Ljava/lang/String;
            // @declaration an instance method of `TM$Task`, member flags 0x0000

        }
